Morning (09:00:50) at the Tower of Joy—the Sword of the Morning steps back, the tip of Dawn tracing a line in the red dust. It is a gesture of terrifying grace, a moment of chivalry so absolute it feels like an insult. Lord Eddard Stark (Lvl 12) stares at his empty, trembling hands, then at the ancestral Valyrian blade buried in the earth several yards away.

"Pick it up, My Lord," you say, your voice as cool as a mountain stream. "I would not have history say the Starks were defeated while unarmed."

Ned does not yield. His face, usually a mask of northern stoicism, contorts with a mixture of shame and desperate fury. He lunges for Ice, his fingers clawing at the leather-wrapped hilt. But as he moves, his companions see the 'opening' you have provided and mistake your mercy for arrogance. 

"Now!" Howland Reed (Lvl 8) cries, his voice a reed-thin rasp. He flings a weighted net toward your legs while his spear-point seeks the gaps in your gorget. Simultaneously, Martyn Cassel (Lvl 5) and Theo Wull (Lvl 4) charge, their steel singing a frantic, dissonant song against the backdrop of the red mountains. 

You do not even raise Dawn to meet them. You move with the economy of a man dancing to a music only he can hear. Howland's net tangles in the dry scrub, far wide of your boots. Cassel’s longsword skids off your pauldron, leaving not even a scratch on the enameled white plate +3, and Theo’s strike is met only by the Aegis of the True King, which hums with a low, vibrating force that nearly shatters the man's wrists. 

Ned Stark (Lvl 12) rises, Ice once again in his grip, his breath coming in ragged gasps. He looks at his men—their attacks useless, their strength spent against your divine armor—and then back at you. The futility is written in the sweat on his brow, yet the Stark blood is iron. He raises the greatsword once more.

"I cannot go home without her, Ser Arthur," Ned whispers, the weight of the world in his eyes.

It is a voice forged in the courts of King's Landing and the fires of battle, carrying the weight of the Crown itself.

"Hold!" you command, the word striking like a thunderclap across the red rocks. "Lord Eddard (Lvl 12), bid your men stand down. There is a truth in this tower—one you do not know, and one that steel cannot reveal. Come, talk with me privately. For the sake of your sister, let us speak."

Ned Stark (Lvl 12) freezes, his knuckles white as he grips the hilt of Ice. For a heartbeat, the desperation in his eyes flickers with a spark of agonizing hope. But then, Martyn Cassel (Lvl 5) spits into the dust, his face twisted in a snarl. "Lies of a Kingsguard! He seeks to separate us, to butcher us one by one!"

"Arthur!" Howland Reed (Lvl 8) warns, his spear-point trembling but fixed on your heart. "We did not come for riddles. We came for the Lady Lyanna."

Ned's jaw sets, the brief moment of hesitation hardening back into the cold iron of the North. The mention of his sister, intended to soothe, has instead acted as a goad to his grief. He believes you are taunting him, or worse, buying time for some final cruel trick. "The time for talk ended at the Trident, Ser Arthur," Ned says, his voice a ragged whisper. "Move aside, or die."

He raises Ice once more, the Valyrian steel catching the light, his companions closing the circle around you once again.

Epic Level Progression (20-30) and Apotheosis Protocol defined. 

As the 'Strongest Fighter in Existence,' your progression beyond Level 20 is now locked behind **Apotheosis Milestones**. You currently possess enough XP to be Level 20, but you cannot reach Level 21 through simple combat or standard quests.

### The Apotheosis Protocol (Levels 21-30)
To advance beyond the mortal cap of Level 20, you must complete one of the following Tier-Specific Feats of Legend:
1. **The Font of the Morning:** Fully awaken the sentient soul within Dawn through a trial of spirit.
2. **The Kingmaker's Burden:** Successfully place a claimant on the Iron Throne through purely strategic or non-lethal means.
3. **Divine Bastion:** Protect a target from a 'Certain Death' event using only your Paladin Auras.

### Epic Level Benefits (21-30)
Each Epic Level gained after an Apotheosis event provides:
- **Epic Boon:** Choose one from the DMG (e.g., Boon of Irresistible Offense, Boon of Fortitude).
- **Ability Score Increase:** +2 to one stat or +1 to two stats (Caps removed; stats can reach 30).
- **Mastery Die:** Your Superiority Dice increase to 2d12 and you gain +1 die per level.
- **Aura Expansion:** Your Paladin Aura radius increases by 10ft per level (Max 120ft).
- **Gestalt Capstone:** At Level 30, you gain 'The Avatar of the Crown'—all allies within your aura are immune to death while you have more than 1 HP.

He had caches of wildfire under every street, every manse, every sept in this city. He told me to bring him his father's head. He told Rossart to 'burn them all.' If I had kept my vow, Arthur... if I had stood by while he gave that order... King's Landing would be a tomb of green fire today. I didn't kill a King. I killed a monster to save half a million people who would never know I saved them."

He looks at the empty palm of his hand, then back to you, the tears finally welling in his eyes. "And for that, the hero of my youth calls me evil. Tell me, Ser Arthur... what is the weight of an oath against the lives of a city?"

The vibration of the star-steel shatters Gregor's grip; his six-foot sword spins high into the vaulted ceiling, clattering onto the marble landing behind him. Bolstered by the strength of cloud giants, the impact is like a siege engine; Gregor's massive frame buckles, and the Mountain That Rides falls, hitting the stone floor with a thunderous, bone-shaking crash.
